Normalize strings: lowercase, remove extra spaces\n    s1 = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', str1.lower().strip())\n    s2 = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', str2.lower().strip())\n\n    # Direct match\n    if s1 == s2:\n        return True\n\n    # Handle common variations\n    variations = [\n        ('limited', 'ltd'),\n        ('ltd.', 'ltd'),\n        ('limited', 'ltd.'),\n        ('incorporated', 'inc'),\n        ('inc.', 'inc'),\n        ('corporation', 'corp'),\n        ('corp.', 'corp'),\n        ('company', 'co'),\n        ('co.', 'co'),\n    ]\n\n    for variant1, variant2 in variations:\n        s1_var = s1.replace(variant1, variant2)\n        s2_var = s2.replace(variant1, variant2)\n        if s1_var == s2_var or s1_var == s2 or s1 == s2_var:\n            return True\n\n    # Set to None if empty or placeholder\n                    if po_str and po_str.lower() not in ['none', 'n/a', '-']:\n                        invoice['po_number'] = po_str\n\n                # Only add if we have at least vendor name and amount\n                if invoice['vendor_name'] and invoice['invoice_amount'] is not None:\n                    invoices.append(invoice)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error extracting PDF data: {e}\")\n        raise\n\n    return invoices\n\ndef load_vendors(xlsx_path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Load vendor data from Excel file.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        df = pd.read_excel(xlsx_path)\n        # Ensure column names are consistent\n        df.columns = df.columns.str.strip()\n        return df\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error loading purchase orders: {e}\")\n        raise\n\ndef find_vendor_by_name(vendor_name: str, vendors_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[pd.Series]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find vendor by name using fuzzy matching.\n    Returns vendor row if found, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    for idx, vendor in vendors_df.iterrows():\n        if fuzzy_match(vendor_name, vendor['Name']):\n            return vendor\n    return None\n\ndef analyze_invoice(invoice: Dict, vendors_df: pd.DataFrame, pos_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyze a single invoice for fraud.\n    Returns fraud record if fraudulent, None if legitimate.\n    Priority order:\n    1. Unknown Vendor\n    2. IBAN Mismatch\n    3. Invalid PO\n    4. Amount Mismatch\n    5. Vendor Mismatch\n    \"\"\"\n    vendor_name = invoice['vendor_name']\n    invoice_amount = invoice['invoice_amount']\n    iban = invoice['iban']\n    po_number = invoice['po_number']\n\n    # 1. Check if vendor is known (with fuzzy matching)\n    vendor = find_vendor_by_name(vendor_name, vendors_df)\n    if vendor is None:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Unknown Vendor'\n        }\n\n    # 2. Check IBAN mismatch\n    if iban and vendor['IBAN'] != iban:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'IBAN Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # If no PO number on invoice, we can't check PO-related fraud\n    if po_number is None:\n        # No fraud detected if vendor and IBAN are valid\n        return None\n\n    # 3. Check if PO exists\n    po = pos_df[pos_df['PO Number'] == po_number]\n    if po.empty:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Invalid PO'\n        }\n\n    po_row = po.iloc[0]\n\n    # 4. Check amount mismatch (tolerance: 0.01)\n    if abs(po_row['Amount'] - invoice_amount) > 0.01:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Amount Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # 5. Check vendor mismatch\n    if po_row['Vendor ID'] != vendor['Vendor ID']:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Vendor Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # No fraud detected\n    return None\n\ndef main():\n    print(\"Starting invoice fraud analysis...\")\n\n    # File paths\n    pdf_path = '/root/invoices.pdf'\n    vendors_path = '/root/vendors.xlsx'\n    pos_path = '/root/purchase_orders.csv'\n    output_path = '/root/fraud_report.json'\n\n    # Extract invoice data\n    print(f\"Extracting invoices from {pdf_path}...\")\n    invoices = extract_invoice_data(pdf_path)\n    print(f\"Extracted {len(invoices)} invoices\")\n\n    # Load vendor and PO data\n    print(f\"Loading vendors from {vendors_path}...\")\n    vendors_df = load_vendors(vendors_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(vendors_df)} vendors\")\n\n    print(f\"Loading purchase orders from {pos_path}...\")\n    pos_df = load_purchase_orders(pos_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(pos_df)} purchase orders\")\n\n    # Analyze each invoice\n    print(\"Analyzing invoices for fraud...\")\n    fraud_records = []\n    for invoice in invoices:\n        fraud = analyze_invoice(invoice, vendors_df, pos_df)\n        if fraud:\n            fraud_records.append(fraud)\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(fraud_records)} fraudulent invoices\")\n\n    # Generate fraud report\n    print(f\"Writing fraud report to {output_path}...\")\n    with open(output_path, 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(fraud_records, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"Fraud analysis complete!\")\n    print(f\"\\nFraud Summary:\")\n    if fraud_records:\n        fraud_counts = {}\n        for record in fraud_records:\n            reason = record['reason']\n            fraud_counts[reason] = fraud_counts.get(reason, 0) + 1\n\n        for reason, count in sorted(fraud_counts.items()):\n            print(f\"  {reason}: {count}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"  No fraud detected\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":133,"cache_read_input_tokens":35980,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":133,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_vrtx_01PV94YUW1PXQBWTshZWgg8T","session_id":"6be5c559-001f-463b-b3d6-60174a156eee","uuid":"19aea3b0-b388-4b4b-beff-53756964b4cc"}

Normalize strings: lowercase, remove extra spaces\n    s1 = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', str1.lower().strip())\n    s2 = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', str2.lower().strip())\n\n    # Direct match\n    if s1 == s2:\n        return True\n\n    # Handle common variations\n    variations = [\n        ('limited', 'ltd'),\n        ('ltd.', 'ltd'),\n        ('limited', 'ltd.'),\n        ('incorporated', 'inc'),\n        ('inc.', 'inc'),\n        ('corporation', 'corp'),\n        ('corp.', 'corp'),\n        ('company', 'co'),\n        ('co.', 'co'),\n    ]\n\n    for variant1, variant2 in variations:\n        s1_var = s1.replace(variant1, variant2)\n        s2_var = s2.replace(variant1, variant2)\n        if s1_var == s2_var or s1_var == s2 or s1 == s2_var:\n            return True\n\n    # Use sequence matcher for fuzzy matching\n    ratio = SequenceMatcher(None, s1, s2).ratio()\n    return ratio >= threshold\n\ndef extract_invoice_data(text_path: str) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract invoice data from converted PDF text file.\n    Returns list of invoices with vendor_name, amount, iban, po_number, page_number.\n    \"\"\"\n    invoices = []\n\n    try:\n        with open(text_path, 'r') as file:\n            content = file.read()\n\n        # Split by invoice pages\n        pages = re.split(r'Invoice Page (\\d+)', content)[1:]  # Skip first empty split\n\n        for i in range(0, len(pages), 2):\n            if i + 1 >= len(pages):\n                break\n\n            page_num = int(pages[i])\n            page_text = pages[i + 1]\n\n            invoice = {\n                'invoice_page_number': page_num,\n                'vendor_name': None,\n                'invoice_amount': None,\n                'iban': None,\n                'po_number': None\n            }\n\n            # Extract Amount (looking for \"Total\")\n            amount_match = re.search(r'Total\\s+\\$?([\\d,]+\\.?\\d*)', page_text)\n            if amount_match:\n                amount_str = amount_match.group(1).replace(',', '')\n                invoice['invoice_amount'] = float(amount_str)\n\n            # Set to None if empty or invalid placeholder\n                if po_str and po_str.lower() not in ['none', 'n/a', '-']:\n                    invoice['po_number'] = po_str\n\n            # Only add if we have at least vendor name and amount\n            if invoice['vendor_name'] and invoice['invoice_amount'] is not None:\n                invoices.append(invoice)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error extracting invoice data: {e}\")\n        raise\n\n    return invoices\n\ndef load_vendors(xlsx_path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Load vendor data from Excel file.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        df = pd.read_excel(xlsx_path)\n        # Ensure column names are consistent - handle different naming conventions\n        df.columns = [col.strip() for col in df.columns]\n\n        # Normalize column names to expected format\n        col_mapping = {}\n        for col in df.columns:\n            col_lower = col.lower()\n            if 'po' in col_lower and 'number' in col_lower:\n                col_mapping[col] = 'PO Number'\n            elif 'vendor' in col_lower and 'id' in col_lower:\n                col_mapping[col] = 'Vendor ID'\n            elif 'amount' in col_lower:\n                col_mapping[col] = 'Amount'\n\n        df = df.rename(columns=col_mapping)\n        return df\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error loading purchase orders: {e}\")\n        raise\n\ndef find_vendor_by_name(vendor_name: str, vendors_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[pd.Series]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Find vendor by name using fuzzy matching.\n    Returns vendor row if found, None otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    for idx, vendor in vendors_df.iterrows():\n        if fuzzy_match(vendor_name, str(vendor['name'])):\n            return vendor\n    return None\n\ndef analyze_invoice(invoice: Dict, vendors_df: pd.DataFrame, pos_df: pd.DataFrame) -> Optional[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Analyze a single invoice for fraud.\n    Returns fraud record if fraudulent, None if legitimate.\n    Priority order:\n    1. Unknown Vendor\n    2. IBAN Mismatch\n    3. Invalid PO\n    4. Amount Mismatch\n    5. Vendor Mismatch\n    \"\"\"\n    vendor_name = invoice['vendor_name']\n    invoice_amount = invoice['invoice_amount']\n    iban = invoice['iban']\n    po_number = invoice['po_number']\n\n    # 1. Check if vendor is known (with fuzzy matching)\n    vendor = find_vendor_by_name(vendor_name, vendors_df)\n    if vendor is None:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Unknown Vendor'\n        }\n\n    # 2. Check IBAN mismatch\n    if iban and str(vendor['iban']) != str(iban):\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'IBAN Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # If no PO number on invoice, we can't check PO-related fraud\n    if po_number is None:\n        # No fraud detected if vendor and IBAN are valid\n        return None\n\n    # 3. Check if PO exists\n    po = pos_df[pos_df['PO Number'] == po_number]\n    if po.empty:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Invalid PO'\n        }\n\n    po_row = po.iloc[0]\n\n    # 4. Check amount mismatch (tolerance: 0.01)\n    if abs(float(po_row['Amount']) - invoice_amount) > 0.01:\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Amount Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # 5. Check vendor mismatch\n    if str(po_row['Vendor ID']) != str(vendor['vendor_id']):\n        return {\n            'invoice_page_number': invoice['invoice_page_number'],\n            'vendor_name': vendor_name,\n            'invoice_amount': invoice_amount,\n            'iban': iban,\n            'po_number': po_number,\n            'reason': 'Vendor Mismatch'\n        }\n\n    # Normalize column names\n    vendors_df.columns = [col.lower().strip() for col in vendors_df.columns]\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(vendors_df)} vendors\")\n    print(f\"Vendor columns: {list(vendors_df.columns)}\")\n\n    print(f\"Loading purchase orders from {pos_path}...\")\n    pos_df = load_purchase_orders(pos_path)\n    print(f\"Loaded {len(pos_df)} purchase orders\")\n    print(f\"PO columns: {list(pos_df.columns)}\")\n\n    # Analyze each invoice\n    print(\"\\nAnalyzing invoices for fraud...\")\n    fraud_records = []\n    for invoice in invoices:\n        fraud = analyze_invoice(invoice, vendors_df, pos_df)\n        if fraud:\n            fraud_records.append(fraud)\n\n    print(f\"Found {len(fraud_records)} fraudulent invoices\")\n\n    #
